Title: Constitutiones Societatis Iesu, cum earum Declarationibus
  (The Constitutions of the Society of Jesus, with their Declarations)
Creator: Society of Jesus (the Constitutions' own text, approved by
  Ignatius of Loyola and the First General Congregation; the
  Declarationes are the order's own official running commentary)
Publisher: Rome: In Collegio Romano eiusdem Societatis, 1606
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: 1606 publication date places this well out of US
  copyright (420 years before this session, 2026); no explicit
  archive.org copyright-status field present on this item, so the
  determination rests on the stated publication date alone, per this
  project's standing PD-by-date rule.
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item constitutionesso00jesu
  (archive.org/details/constitutionesso00jesu), fetched directly (curl,
  raw bytes) from the item's own _djvu.txt full-text file, 2026-09-24,
  this session.
Content note: This is the Latin original of the Society's own governing
  document - the language in which Ignatius and the early Society
  themselves wrote and used it. The only English translation this
  world's own dossier could locate (George Ganss, S.J., 1970/1996,
  Institute of Jesuit Sources) is in copyright and lending-restricted;
  per this project's own second-witness rule (cic/texts/INTAKE.md), this
  file is PRIMARY content for this world, not a second witness, since no
  English rendering of this work currently exists in this corpus.
  Includes the Primum ac generale examen (pp. 3-48 of the printed
  volume) and a 68-page index; ~394 pages total.
Provenance note: Body text unaltered including its own OCR errors (the
  title page itself shows visible period-typeface OCR noise, e.g.
  "Inftitutum" for "Institutum," "f" misread for long-s, a known and
  expected pattern for pre-19th-century typefaces, disclosed rather than
  silently corrected). Not independently spot-checked for OCR quality
  beyond the opening pages this session.

BOSTON  PUBLIC  LIBRARY
